# Base Game V2 audit

Coverage checkpoint: 213 of 287 distinct designs (213 of 340 official catalog entries conservatively represented; 281 ability rows; 213 designs have open engine gaps or verification tasks, including the 7 original entries; 0 identity/edition uncertainties; 9 provisional exact representative IDs (yellow/orange Pills!, XVI. The Tower, XVIII. The Moon, Fly, Hanger, Keeper Head, Mom's Dead Hand and Mom's Eye and Cursed Fatty); 53 additional identical catalog copies require reconciliation). Last completed batch: 043. Engine support is pinned to [Sprint 14 completion `0e668c1`](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/commit/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c). Tests are inspected, not executed in this audit. Complete coverage requires a reconciled catalog, a disposition for each entry (including cards without an ability), and an engine/test reference for each supported ability.

[Official set index](https://foursouls.com/cards/) · [Set filter](https://foursouls.com/card-search/?origin=b2)

## Cross-cutting audit: player observations and UI information boundaries

Source-inspected against the same pinned Sprint 14 commit. This is a rules/agent-interface gap, not a claim that the existing trusted demo bots intentionally cheat. No card entries are added by this review.

- **Privileged agent API:** all three documented bots accept `choose_command(game: Game)`; the README example passes the full game into the bot. `Game` exposes mutable state, all decks/discards/covered slot data, raw log, RNG, stack effect instances and the pending-trigger queue. Opponent hands and deck order can be read directly; engine objects can also be mutated. Current bot strategies select legal commands without intentionally looking at hidden cards. - **Confirmed CLI wrong-viewer leak:** `render_player_row` prints public hand counts, but `render_hand` prints active player's full hand and their loot quota; `render_board` always calls it. Legal actions are generated for the priority holder, including off-turn loot. During P1's turn with P2 holding priority the board shows P1's private hand and P1's allowance next to P2's actions. No viewer argument, screen handoff or transcript isolation in the CLI. Merely changing active player to priority holder fixes the wrong hand, not shared-terminal privacy. [foursouls/cli/render.py L73-L114](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/blob/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c/foursouls/cli/render.py#L73-L114); [foursouls/cli/render.py L298-L331](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/blob/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c/foursouls/cli/render.py#L298-L331); [foursouls/cli/controller.py L27-L74](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/blob/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c/foursouls/cli/controller.py#L27-L74); [foursouls/cli/app.py L164-L212](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/blob/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c/foursouls/cli/app.py#L164-L212); [foursouls/rulesets/common/legality.py L34-L108](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/blob/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c/foursouls/rulesets/common/legality.py#L34-L108); [tests/acceptance/test_sprint12_acceptance.py L111-L162](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/blob/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c/tests/acceptance/test_sprint12_acceptance.py#L111-L162).
- **Raw event disclosure:** `CardDrawn` includes full CardRef (card_id and instance_id); DrawLoot1Effect and all-player loot draw log it, as does monster reward draw. `StepResult.events` returns raw unfiltered events and `Game.log` is directly exposed. The CLI's CardDrawn renderer correctly hides the card name from its shared narrative, but this is presentation-only; the event has no audience metadata. Do not blanket-redact public treasure acquisitions or publicly revealed cards. Keep internal dispatch/replay events privileged, project public summaries and owner-only/private reveal payloads to each viewer. - **Legal-action and choice exposure:** `get_legal_actions(game)` is a useful engine-owned legality adapter, but no viewer/actor parameter. Loot action keys, metadata, description and command include card identity; give these only to the authorized decision owner, not a shared opponent/spectator observation. Existing `AbilityEngine.choose_target` checks chooser and candidate, but the CLI/common command API has no pending-choice presentation or choice command; raw queue contains choice data. Decisions need their own owner, which is not necessarily active player or priority holder. `Game.step(command)` verifies membership in current legal commands but does not authenticate caller identity; a player-facing boundary must bind actor and decision token. [foursouls/engine/actions.py L48-L102](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/blob/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c/foursouls/engine/actions.py#L48-L102); [foursouls/engine/actions.py L199-L217](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/blob/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c/foursouls/engine/actions.py#L199-L217); [foursouls/engine/ability_engine.py L136-L166](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/blob/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c/foursouls/engine/ability_engine.py#L136-L166); [foursouls/model/commands.py](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/blob/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c/foursouls/model/commands.py); [tests/unit/test_legal_actions.py L126-L220](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/blob/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c/tests/unit/test_legal_actions.py#L126-L220); [tests/unit/test_trigger_pipeline.py L78-L140](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/blob/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c/tests/unit/test_trigger_pipeline.py#L78-L140).
- **Additional presentation/timing blockers:** CLI board shows no deck counts/discard contents, only top stack label and item names (not counters or item charge states); raw effects can contain live deck references. Shop UI and action metadata hardcode TREASURE_COST. CLI auto-passes START/END even when meaningful actions exist, and auto-passes the acting player after a stack push under certain conditions; those paths can skip valid responses and should be replaced by engine-owned quiescence that passes only when no meaningful action exists. Seeded RNG and deterministic demo-deck construction are useful for tests/replay, but seed/RNG state must not be exposed to competitive agents (CLI prompts for seed; shared seeded hotseat is not adversarially private). [foursouls/cli/render.py L73-L99](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/blob/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c/foursouls/cli/render.py#L73-L99); [foursouls/cli/render.py L137-L187](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/blob/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c/foursouls/cli/render.py#L137-L187); [foursouls/engine/actions.py L269-L292](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/blob/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c/foursouls/engine/actions.py#L269-L292); [foursouls/cli/app.py L52-L85](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/blob/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c/foursouls/cli/app.py#L52-L85); [foursouls/cli/app.py L152-L212](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/blob/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c/foursouls/cli/app.py#L152-L212); [foursouls/cli/controller.py L96-L101](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/blob/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c/foursouls/cli/controller.py#L96-L101); [foursouls/engine/stack.py L10-L38](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/blob/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c/foursouls/engine/stack.py#L10-L38); [foursouls/rulesets/common/effects.py L17-L38](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/blob/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c/foursouls/rulesets/common/effects.py#L17-L38); [ROADMAP.md L262-L275](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/blob/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c/ROADMAP.md#L262-L275).
- **Required architecture (proposal):** keep Game as trusted referee; expose immutable detached per-viewer observations (public board including hand/deck counts, public discard/stack/state; own hand; explicitly authorized private inspection results; no raw Game/Effect/RNG objects, secret card IDs or nested mutable references). Separate viewer, active player, priority holder and decision chooser. Project legal actions and private decisions only to their owner; submit opaque action/decision tokens to authoritative validation. Separate public and per-viewer event projections from internal logs. Continuous private information permission is a read-only projection of current top card, independent of stack activation/priority, refreshed on deck changes and revoked on turn/source changes. A one-shot look produces a private snapshot, not permanent access to later top cards. Public reveal is a separate audience. Preserve legitimately learned information without leaking new hidden state. Trusted debug/replay remains explicitly privileged. For untrusted Python agents a DTO alone is not a sandbox: isolate process/service as needed.
- **Acceptance criteria:** two-player off-turn CLI hand and quota; own hand/other hand count; no raw opponent hand/deck identity in observation/actions/events/serialization (including nested references and stable IDs); distinct observations for states differing only in secrets are identical to unauthorized viewer, with same authorized public data; raw draw event retained internally but opponent gets count only; public reveal vs private look; authorized temporary/continuous views (There's Options own turn incl. off-priority, current top changes, loss/transfer, end turn, empty deck); chooser-only private hand candidates for Incubus/Dead Bird/Tarot Cloth; wrong actor/stale decision rejected; no mutation of engine via observation; no RNG seed/state exposure to competitive agent; correct off-turn priority and no auto-pass of meaningful responses. Keep these tests separate from trusted deterministic bot acceptance tests.
- **Roadmap association:** Sprint 20 private deck inspection, Sprint 21 hand transfer, Sprint 23 decision/trigger presentation, Sprint 25 static permissions, Sprint 17 priority/quiescence, Sprint 18 simulation runner. The cross-cutting observation boundary and privacy regressions are not explicitly assigned as a complete deliverable in the pinned roadmap; recommend foundation before fair agent comparisons (Sprint 19) and before private inspection mechanics. Existing IMPL_STATUS already acknowledges hand/deck visibility gaps; this review supplies concrete code evidence and a correction to its broad description of CLI hand rendering. [IMPL_STATUS.md L85-L89](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/blob/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c/IMPL_STATUS.md#L85-L89); [IMPL_STATUS.md L123-L124](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/blob/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c/IMPL_STATUS.md#L123-L124); [IMPL_STATUS.md L252-L253](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/blob/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c/IMPL_STATUS.md#L252-L253).
- **Scope:** all three README-listed agents, current CLI app/controller/render, legal action adapter, game loop/step result, commands, priority, pending choice, state/zone/reference/stack/RNG/log/event definitions and relevant existing acceptance tests inspected. Source inspection only; no test execution. GitHub code search returned no results and connector lacks a pinned recursive tree listing, so cannot certify every unadvertised frontend or test file. Old human_cli/run_cli paths from historical PRs are absent at baseline. No engine or roadmap changes in this audit.
**Catalog counting clarification (batch 026):** The [official set index](https://foursouls.com/cards/) lists 340 physical/catalog entries; the [default Base Game V2 search](https://foursouls.com/card-search/?origin=b2) shows 287 with identical cards grouped. Maintain one ability breakdown per distinct card design, not per identical physical copy. The representative-entry checkpoint is a conservative count of individually reviewed representative entries, not exact physical-card coverage. Against the 287 grouped results the distinct-design coverage is the current checkpoint numerator divided by 287, subject to full ID reconciliation. Track the remaining 53 duplicate catalog entries through a separate multiplicity/alias reconciliation, not duplicate ability audits. Demo deck composition is not a verified full Base Game V2 deck: [app.py L65-L66](https://github.com/alepiaz/FourSouls/blob/0e668c1a524288e3fdebb24eca03f9471178641c/foursouls/cli/app.py#L65-L66). For this batch all six card identities are confirmed by official grouped catalog and submitted image; representative IDs for 2/3/4 cents and Nickel are confirmed by catalog image filenames, individual page fetches failed. No tests executed.

Respect all other action restrictions (Trinity Shield), priority/phase legality and end-of-turn reset; no arbitrary finite sentinel. Test tapped cost, cancellation, source already destroyed, gained Eternal (Eden), transfer while pending, prevented/replaced destruction, off-turn repeated plays and turn expiration. Test destruction, death penalty, duplicate copies, acquisition paths, transfer, prevention ordering and all damage types. | [Bomb! (`b2-bomb`)](https://foursouls.com/cards/b2-bomb/) | Normal loot; choose player or monster on stack entry | Selected player or exact live monster incarnation | Deal 1 ability damage | **Partially supported** — registered BOMB! with correct amount, factory routes player/monster targets to legacy damage primitives; existing Sprint 10 acceptance tests cover both player amounts and Bomb monster damage/target enumeration (not direct Gold Bomb monster regression). Damage mutates HP immediately within loot resolution, bypasses common pending-damage replacement and universal damage events; monster selector/effect stores slot only, so replacement occupant may be hit; game loop checks combat attacker death but lacks universal player death handling. Reuse Razor Blade/Mr. Boom/Golden Razor Blade pending damage lifecycle, exact incarnation target, independent damage response window, prevention, actual damage classification and death/reward dispatch. Test player/monster, source canceled, target changes slot occupant, lethal off-turn/non-attacker, Dry Baby/Yum Heart/Host Hat/Fanny Pack, no combat classification, physical discard once. | [Guppy's Hairball (`b2-guppys_hairball`)](https://foursouls.com/cards/b2-guppys_hairball/) | Mandatory would-take-damage trigger; non-attack roll and result-dependent prevention | Exact pending damage instance targeting controller | On six prevent one point of that damage | **Missing mechanics** — absent; DamageWouldBeDealt and synchronous replacement delta/pooled shields exist; no universal pending damage ID or respondable would-take trigger/roll continuation bound to original event. Combat miss replacement called before damage is pushed, combat hit marks immediately, ordinary ability damage bypasses pipeline. Trigger once per damage instance per active copy, not per point; roll 6 reduces that specific pending amount by one (no future shield), keep original source and residual damage; zero residual produces no taken-damage event; no trigger for HP payment/direct kill/zero damage. Reuse Broken Ankh's pending-event context and roll continuation, Soul Heart prevention primitive; do not model as synchronous RNG replacement or reuse triggering attack roll. Stale/canceled original damage must not affect another event; multiple sources and competing replacement timing verify; avoid repeated trigger when same event resumes. Test all final results, rerolls, 1/3 damage, full prevention, lethal residual, combat/loot/game damage, off-turn, multiple copies, original canceled during roll, source destruction/transfer, subsequent unrelated damage and Bloody Penny only on actual death. | [Lost Soul (`b2-lost_soul`)](https://foursouls.com/cards/b2-lost_soul/) | Mandatory triggered ability when this enters play; distinct from Trinket resolution | Exact source item incarnation; soul value 1 | Become a soul; cease being an item | **Missing mechanics** — absent; do not substitute alternate-art As wording or grant soul directly on loot resolution. First resolve Trinket to item, dispatch enter-play ability, put conversion trigger on stack with normal response/cancel windows. Reuse The Bone/The Chest conversion primitive, not destruction/reward or synchronous entry replacement. Item/soul lists and ZoneIdentityRegistry available; existing item entry hook treasure-only synchronous, monster rewards append soul and check len(souls) only in combat. Add atomic source-bound conversion preserving physical identity and loot origin, remove item characteristics/subscriptions/counters, prevent stale trigger converting destroyed/reentered item; if entry ability canceled remains item until other effect moves it. Source destruction before conversion does not remove pending ability but conversion cannot find original incarnation. Control transfer before resolution requires preserving same object and verify recipient rule (proposed current controller retains soul since this becomes a soul, no explicit give); do not bind to arbitrary replacement item. After successful conversion no longer item-targetable or death penalty item, becomes soul-targetable; route destruction to loot discard and allow recycle/replay, unlike retired bonus souls. Existing DestroySoulEffect drops ref without destination; monster soul path duplicates discard and soul. Universal total-soul-value win check between stack resolutions for all players including off-turn, character contribution and modified threshold; no win merely on item entry or pending trigger. Test cancel loot vs cancel trigger, destruction/transfer before conversion, same physical ref reentry, copy characteristics interaction, fourth soul and off-turn win, source-appropriate discard, Judgement/Mom's Shovel and death penalty eligibility. Need generic event-bound damage duplication with independent recipient prevention, stable physical/incarnation IDs, original-event correlation and no repeat per source/event; amount from correct damage stage, not monster HP difference (positive damage at zero HP possible), no duplication for HP payment/direct kill or zero damage. Directional selector from active player's right in seating order, not attacker, killer or source controller; current next_player_id provides circular order only; explicitly document clockwise convention, use deterministic left/right inverse mapping. Check 3+ player-only icon/catalog eligibility (community reference reports both restricted; screenshot icon small and official page text lacks metadata); generic setup filter and encountered-card replacement required if confirmed, no arbitrary two-player targeting fallback. Confirmed baseline defects: combat hit emits damage amount before MonsterInPlay.take_damage pooled shield absorption (even full prevention can emit positive DamageDealt); direct loot/ability monster damage emits no actual-damage event; EventTargetIsAbilitySourceCondition accepts only would-damage/death not DamageDealt; shared DealDamageDefinition fixed positive amount and builder drops source; replacement pipeline returns one amount and cannot add independent damage target. Reuse universal damage from Swallowed Penny/Hairball, Black Bony provenance, Tower ordered damage and nested deaths. Test nonlethal/lethal/overkill, damage to correct neighbor when off-turn player uses Bomb, full/partial prevention on either recipient, independent prevention/cancellation, direct kill no damage, stale or covered monster, multiple copies, wraparound seating, simultaneous deaths, source provenance and 7-cent reward, 3/4 players and restricted-card filtering. | [Mulliboom (`b2-mulliboom`)](https://foursouls.com/cards/b2-mulliboom/) | Mandatory own-death trigger before reward | Active player chooses a player including self; active player is explicit damage dealer | Deal 3 noncombat damage to chosen player; HP1/DC2/ATK4; gain 6 cents reward; no soul | **Primitives available only** — absent; printed 4! is numeric ATK4 with emphasis, no separate keyword. Source-specific MonsterDied condition, ChosenPlayerSelector and DealDamageEffect exist; builder stores target/amount/type only and direct HP mutation bypasses distinct damage stack/dispatch; cannot attribute actual damage dealer. Unlike Boom Fly/Black Bony (monster deals) or Tower (game-sourced), active player deals the damage even if someone else killed Mulliboom. Keep ability source, game-controlled trigger, explicit damage-dealer player and recipient separate.  Monster registration stores controller_id=active_player_id at entry and returns early for live identities; turn advance does not refresh it. Chosen selectors use that stored ID; game-controlled triggers must instead capture the active player at trigger scheduling (not treat the monster as a player-controlled object), including ordering and choice ownership. Choose target at stack entry, damage resolves independently and is preventable; lethal target death through universal pipeline, pre-reward trigger and damage stage ordering must follow rules (verify exact reward timing relative to newly queued damage). No damage on prevented monster death/canceled trigger; do not drop already earned reward on active-player death. Test self/other, off-turn killer, active-player changes since spawn, player-attributed damage, no combat-damage trigger, shield/cap/Hairball/Swallowed Penny, lethal off-turn and active damage, 4 ATK on miss, 6 cents reward.
